  - Confidence: high

**2026-07-15 — TERMINAL CHART REPAIR (client/ only): 16 confirmed market-chart bugs — view management, ET axis, overlay pane geometry, bar-index X mapping everywhere, mobile header, data-hook races**
- **"Pan/zoom dead" was NOT an event-interception bug.** Live-probe forensics (wrapping lw-charts internals via a DEV `window.__ttChart` hook): pointer events reached the pane canvas, `handleScroll/handleScale` options were enabled, and the gesture pipeline (`startScrollTime`→`scrollTimeTo`) fired. TWO real causes: (1) lw-charts starts a drag-scroll at the SECOND pressed-move (`_startScrollingPos` set on move 1, scroll starts+anchors on move 2), so a synthetic 2-move CDP drag nets ZERO pan — /classic "worked" only because CandlestickChart implements its own absolute-delta drag. Multi-move streams (= real mice) pan fine. (2) The chart OPENED unusable: `fitContent()` on the whole dataset clamps to `minBarSpacing` 0.5px/bar (a smear where pan looks inert), and on 15m the fit puts logical 0 in view → the `range.from<20` deep-history trigger AUTO-FIRED a 163k-bar download on every open. RULE: never `fitContent()` a deep-history dataset — land on the live edge with a bounded window (`fitRecent`, DEFAULT_VIEW_BARS=240) and require `candles.length > 50` before honoring the left-edge trigger.
- **Structural-change classifier in the candles effect (TerminalLiveChart):** every setData now classifies PREPEND (old first bar binary-searched in the new array → shift the saved logical range by its index — robust to interior gap-fills, exact for pure suffixes) / APPEND-OR-SAME (same first bar, last ≥ prev → leave the view alone) / TRIM-SLIDE (MAX_CANDLES cap slid: new first bar binary-searched in the OLD `series.data()` → negative shift, skipped when hugging the live edge) / REPLACEMENT (everything else → force `fitRecent` REGARDLESS of didFitRef — this is what stranded interval switches in the past). Verified: deep load 2,191→200,000 bars with the viewed time range byte-identical before/after; 18s reconcile soak while scrolled back = 0 drift; rollover at the 200k cap while viewing Mar 2026 = no snap.
- **Interval-switch race fixed on BOTH sides:** useTerminalData gained a fetch-GENERATION token (`genRef` bumped on symbol/interval/reload; every async fetch captures it pre-await and discards stale responses) + the reset effect clears `candles`/`lastBarRef` so old-interval ticks can't mutate mid-switch. CRITICAL COMPANION FIX: the live `bar`/`tick` handlers must NOT seed a 1-bar dataset while the switch fetch is in flight (`if (!lastBarRef.current) return`) — a forming-bar broadcast otherwise creates a 1-bar chart, fitContent puts logical 0 in view (auto-fires the deep load), and the arriving fetch classifies as a PREPEND that preserves the degenerate 6-bar view. Found live: rapid 15m→60m→1m→5m left a 6-bar viewport + a full 15m deep-history download.
- **WS `bar` messages are NESTED**: the server broadcasts `{type:"bar", resolution, bar:{symbol,time,open,...}}` (live-bars.ts:312, mw-reader.ts) — the client read flat `msg.symbol/msg.time` so the handler was 100% dead. Read `msg.bar` with a flat fallback (the REST `/api/live-bars` path also nests). Also `data_updated` now merges from the loaded floor (`reconcile(floorRef)`) instead of `fetchCandles()` — the old path silently WIPED lazily-loaded deep history back to the 10-day window and could blank the chart on an empty response. Reconcile keeps the local forming bar ONLY when it IS the current wall-clock bucket (stale forming bars no longer overwrite server rows for closed buckets).
- **Overlay canvas geometry: use the PANE, not the host.** `timeScale().width()` = pane width (host minus price axis, 468 vs 540 here), `height()` = time-axis strip. The yellowbox `logToX` used host width → every box stretched/shifted right ~15% (verified fix: our edge x now matches `timeToCoordinate` of the session's first candle within 1px). All overlay layers now clip to `(0,0,paneW,paneBottom)` so nothing paints over the axes (regime ribbon moved above the time axis too). Milk zones ported to the SAME bar-index snapping (binary search + interpolation between neighbours + per-interval extrapolation beyond the ends) — the old time-linear `estX` was wrong on a bar-index axis AND conflated its `-1` "can't map" sentinel with legitimately negative off-left coordinates (the full-width stale-zone bug).
- **HMR hazards while other agents run:** (a) adding hooks inside a custom hook changes the hook COUNT across a hot update → React "order of Hooks" crash-remount — dev-only, a full reload clears it; (b) agents editing `shared/` invalidate `@shared` imports → TradingTerminal remounts and RE-READS `mwb_settings.interval`, which the hidden engine rewrites — the terminal can silently flip interval mid-session (looked exactly like my bug; wasn't); (c) verifying an Edit through Vite HMR can run STALE module code — version-stamp a debug marker before trusting a "the fix didn't work" observation (the seam-note fix was live-verified only after v-stamping).
- **Seam note (consumes the server agent's new field):** `servedFloorTs` probed defensively from `/api/data/served-range` then `/api/data/gaps` (endpoint went live mid-task — the defensive probe found it). Note draws when 1m + left edge in view + (loaded floor ≤ servedFloorTs, OR fullyLoaded — the MAX_CANDLES 200k cap ≈ 7 months of 1m means the user can hit the client cap far above the server floor; both are "1m ends here, switch to 5m").
- Misc fixes verified: `Math.max(...200k-element array)` stack overflow in terminal.tsx zone upload → loop; footprint effect no longer does a full setData + whole-history refetch per bar rollover when OFF (idempotent early-out) and refetches when ON only when candle coverage extends (2 fetches/16min observed vs 1/rollover before); signal cutoff extends to the loaded-candle floor (1,701 of 1,771 MES/5m signals were silently hidden by the 30-day cap — markers verified rendering on Mar 2026 history); vector highlight re-applies on interval change (5m teal after a 15m→5m switch); ≤640px header wraps with a scrollable tab row (Portfolio reachable at 390×844, dropdown 330px wide, toggles inside the viewport); yellowbox persistent bands merge into cross-session RUNS keyed on geometry with the most-recent session's label + pixel-collision label dedupe; NewsTicker decodes HTML entities via a detached textarea. NOT DONE (timeboxed out): the /classic first-paint + scrubber-overlap minor.
- VERIFIED: tsc 0; `npm run build` 0; live browser pass — rapid interval switching lands on the live edge with pure 300s deltas (no old-interval mixing), Reload recovers, ET axis + crosshair ("Jul 15, 26, 07:30 ET"), price-axis drag → overlays track, deep-scroll + reconcile stable, mobile geometry probed via DOM rects.
- Confidence: high

**2026-07-15 — ICT-STRATEGY BACKTEST (theicttrader.com / Fadi Zeidan): standalone 7-strategy engine + full-history workbook + combo analysis + past-month trade charts**
- MISSION: mechanical translations of the 5 studied articles into `shared/ict-engine.ts` (PURE — imports only firing/types; 7 strategies: high-prob Order Block, Fair Value Gap, Breaker(+Unicorn flag), Liquidity-Sweep/turtle-soup, MSS+OTE, C.E. wick reversal, Mitigation block; EVERY threshold a named constant in `ICT_CONST`), harness `scripts/ict-backtest.ts` (5m/15m/60m — 1m skipped per the quality lesson; RTH+ETH with session + kill-zone context columns, kill zones reported but never gating; ANALYSIS ONLY — zero live-engine changes), workbook `ict-backtest.xlsx`: README / All ICT Trades / 7 solo sheets + Unicorn / ICT+ICT combo sheets (n≥15) / ICT×existing-engine sheets / Rare Combos / Comparison, every sheet with an ALL-hours stats row + a kill-zone-filtered row (real COUNTIFS/SUMIFS with cached results; Max DD = JS literal, marked).
- **Engine/harness split that made ICT testable:** the engine emits SETUPS (entry kind+level, stop, TPs, invalidation level); ALL fills resolve harness-side on 1m bars (limit fills AT the level inside a 30-bar retest window; SL-first same-bar; 17:00 ET settle; one open trade per strategy-interval accepted chronologically by FILL time). Session levels (prior-day H/L, Asia/London/prior-NY H/L) are INJECTED as time-windowed `LiquidityLevel` rows computed from 1m and armed only when each window completes — the engine stays calendar-free and unit-testable on synthetic bars (42 plain-assert tests incl. a truncation NO-LOOKAHEAD test: full-run vs sliced-run setups byte-identical).
- **Two liquidity views per swing:** WICK-consumed (sweep detection, target pools) vs CLOSE-consumed (structure breaks for the MSS/OB shift condition). One set with flags drifts — keep two capped arrays (200 most-recent per side; bounded cost, documented judgment call).
- **FVG "two 50%s" trap:** the gap's own consequent encroachment and the wicks-article impulse fib (c2.high→c1.low) can sit on EITHER side of each other; "entry at the gap edge, invalid past the impulse-50%" kills every setup where the fib sits above the gap. Coherent-in-all-geometries rule: entry = the SHALLOWER of the two, pre-fill invalidation = the DEEPER, checked on 1m bar OPENS only (any continuous path through the deeper level fills first).
- Test-fixture FP gotcha: a hand-made 0.9 body vs a 1.5×0.6 threshold compares 0.9000000000000057 > 0.899… — keep synthetic bodies clearly away from thresholds; and "isolated" fixtures legitimately fire OTHER strategies (my OB fixture fired mitigation off a warmup bar until bodies were shrunk) — run detector tests with the engine's `strategies:` filter.
- **Excel tab names:** combo keys >31 chars truncate into COLLIDING and TRAILING-SPACE tabs (Excel repair prompt). Compress via tight codes before slicing ("Brkr+CE+FVG+Mit+MSS+OB+Swp" = 26 chars for all seven) and strip trailing space/dot after ANY slice. Also: a Comparison sheet ranked purely by expectancy puts n=1–6 rare-combo noise on top — rank n≥15 classes first, rare below.
- RESULTS (full history 2019-08→2026-07-15, post-repair DB — the known-worst bar MES 5m 2026-05-25 20:45Z VERIFIED GONE before the final run): 204,047 setups → 138,272 accepted trades. Kill-zone filtering helps most (Breaker→1.35, OB→1.11) but NOT Sweep (→1.01). Best sizeable combos: MSS-OTE+Sweep n=74 +4.16/PF 1.74, FVG+Sweep n=579 +1.46/1.38, FVG+OB n=1,251 +1.03/1.34; ICT×existing: FVG×Confluence n=367 +1.05/1.31, Sweep×Confluence n=116 win 55%. No slippage/commission — README says so loudly.

**2026-07-15 — DB REPAIR (355 rows, 5 classes) + the Yahoo forming-bar writer killed + audit watchdog + yellowbox endpoint fixes (mwml reload/heal/next-day bands/low-conf/straddle) + serving-cap seam**
- **OFF-GRID WRITER ROOT CAUSE (`server/routes.ts:517` `yahooBackfillSymbol`, auto-run per startup at routes.ts:3596):** Yahoo's chart API appends the current FORMING bar stamped at `regularMarketTime` — the wall-clock SECOND of the fetch — with V=0 and O=H=L=C≈last price; `mapQuotes` passed it straight to an unguarded `onConflictDoNothing` insert. Fingerprint that identified it: rows only on 5/15/60 (the fetched intervals, never 1m), ES+MES pairs at the SAME second (parallel fire-and-forget loops both fetching ES=F), 60m rows ~9s before 5m/15m (fetch order), timestamps = server-restart times. **FORENSIC TRICK: use the AUTOINCREMENT id-space** — each off-grid row was the TAIL of a consecutive-id bulk-insert run (hours of grid-aligned history + one now-stamped snapshot), and identical volumes under ES and MES betrayed one upstream fetch stored under both symbols. FIX: `validateBar` (grid+ghost+malformed) **+ `isSessionOpen`** in the yahoo upsert — the session gate matters because Yahoo ALSO emits on-grid V=1..61 maintenance-hour (17:00 ET) 60m prints that grid/ghost checks pass; the first fix round missed them and the startup backfill promptly RE-INSERTED 6 rows my repair had just deleted. RULE: after deleting bad rows, re-run the scan AFTER the next server restart — any auto-startup ingest will loudly tell you which fingerprints it can still write.
- **Lowest-level write guard = a SQLite BEFORE INSERT trigger (db.ts `trg_cached_candles_grid_guard`):** logs off-grid rows to `candle_write_rejects` then `RAISE(IGNORE)`s them — catches EVERY writer (drizzle, raw prepared stmts, scripts, future code). GOTCHA: RAISE(IGNORE) abandons the REMAINDER of a multi-row INSERT (rows after the offending one are silently skipped), so every bulk path must still pre-filter in JS (yahoo/import-csv/import-mw-dump/import-full now do; live-bars/mw-reader already did) — the trigger is a single-stray-row backstop, not a bulk filter. Trigger is DROP+CREATE at startup (idempotent, definition stays current).
- **AUDIT FREEZE ROOT CAUSE (last_audit_ts frozen 2026-07-13 22:15 ET for ~19h):** not a dead timer — audits are HELLO-GATED. `last_audit_ts` is stamped only on study hello / productive backfill / the hourly tick, and the hourly tick iterates ONLY connected LiveBarRelay studies. When the study socket dropped and MW never re-helloed, `studies` was empty → nothing audited, nothing re-armed — while the SEPARATE TickRelay feed kept live bars flowing, so it looked healthy. FIX: 5-min WATCHDOG in gap-audit.ts — stale>30min WITH a live study → loud log + immediate re-arm (stamp+audit+dispatch); stale>6h with NO study → loud log (1h throttle/key); hourly tick got per-study try/catch (one SQLITE_BUSY must not abort the whole tick). `getAuditStaleness()` exposed via GET /api/mw/sync-status (`audit:[{symbol,resolution,lastAuditTs,staleSec,studyConnected,stale}]` + `candleWriteRejects:{total,lastHour}`). Verified live: MES:5 connected+fresh(29s), MES:1/15/60 stale 11.3h+disconnected flagged.
- **In-memory queues do NOT survive the restart you do at the end of the task:** `reconcilePending` (gap-audit) is per-process — the class-6 MW reconcile queued mid-task had to be RE-QUEUED after the final restart. If a repair step queues server-side work, queue it AFTER the last restart (or persist it).
- **Yellowbox endpoint v3 (`YB_CACHE_VER=3`, per-day `import_hash` + `low_conf` columns):** (a) mwml registry reloads on a data/reference name/mtime/size signature change (verified live: 7158→10733→7158 figures across a copy/delete, no restart) and each day's cache row stores a hash of its ELIGIBLE figure set — new/updated .mwml regenerates exactly the affected days (hash mismatch = uncached) instead of staying frozen; (b) traded=0 (holiday) rows HEAL: warm path re-checks `EXISTS bars(res 1|5)` in the session span and recomputes when a backfill has landed (verified: planted a traded=0 marker on 2026-03-25 → request recomputed identical box 6637.11-6657.16 → re-froze traded=1); (c) **eligible-set fix — intra-day-drawn mwml figures were invisible on EVERY day** (`sessionDayKey===dayKey` failed for later days, the 09:30 cutoff failed for their own day): now day K uses the FRESHEST figure-day with drawStart<cutoff(K) — pre-open sets serve their own day as before, intra-day sets serve from the NEXT day onward until superseded (verified: 2026-03-23..27 now serve 9-21 [m] bands each, previously ~0); (d) <10 prior trading days → day not emitted, 10-19 → `lowConfidence:true` JSON flag; (e) auto persistent bands STRADDLING the pre-open price are kept and classified to the side holding more profile volume (summed over the scan window's DayProfile buckets) instead of being dropped by the strict above/below filters.
- **1m serving-cap seam exposed for the client:** GET /api/data/cached-continuous now returns `servedFloorTs` (oldest timestamp actually served under the per-res row cap, null when uncapped); same field + `cap` added to GET /api/data/gaps/:symbol/:resolution; new lightweight GET /api/data/served-range/:symbol/:resolution → `{symbol,resolution,cap,servedFloorTs,earliestTs,latestTs,total}`. Client agent: consume `servedFloorTs`.
- **DB repair (backup FIRST: better-sqlite3 `db.backup()` → data/app.db.backup-pre-repair-2026-07-15, 1.03GB, safe against the live server's WAL):** class 1 roll-basis native 5m in 1m-gap buckets >1% off BOTH adjacent closes, 2019-09..2020-04 = **102** (the audit's ~160 came from a last-derived-close basis measure; the prescribed adjacent-neighbor predicate converges to a fixpoint in ONE pass — verified by iterating); class 2 same predicate elsewhere = **17** (incl. the explicitly-named MLK 2026-01-19 18:05Z row which is only 15pt displaced but starts AFTER the 13:00 ET holiday close, V=2, no 1m — named rows may need their own justification when the class predicate misses them); class 3 off-grid V=0 O=H=L=C snapshots = **195** (69 was the MES-only scan count; ES had 123 more + drift); class 4 :30-anchored 60m dupes = **25 of 40** (all 20 MES + 5 ES 17:30Z rows with :00 coverage; the other 15 ES rows are the ONLY data for those hours — kept); class 5 = 2 1m maintenance rows + **6 derived buckets deleted** (their only 1m died — deriveForDeletedOneMin semantics inline) + **8** older 60m 21:00Z prints (scan said 4 = MES-only; ES mirrored them). TOTAL **355**. Class 6 (2025-12-12 06-07Z 1m phantom) queued via POST /api/data/reconcile-ranges/MES/1 — pending study hello. Class 7 live-edge buckets re-derived via POST /api/data/derive/MES and verified bar-exact vs 1m recomputation. Post-repair re-scan: every class ZERO.
- VERIFIED: tsc 0; build 0; 97/97 engine tests; server restarted via preview tools (twice — the second forced by the 5b re-insertion discovery, then final); startup yahoo-backfill logs show the write guard dropping 35-148 rows/chunk; 10-min live-tick watch = zero new off-grid rows; sync-status staleness + seam endpoints exercised live.
- Confidence: high

**2026-07-14 — QUALITY GATE + MONTE-CARLO EXITS: data-driven class filter in the LIVE engine, per-class calibrated exits, quality-over-quantity workbook, no-jargon display names**
- MISSION (user: "Quality over quantity — I don't need a million trades, I need trades that will win"): (1) `shared/quality-gate.ts` — a GENERATED, checked-in config (regenerated by every `scripts/fact-engine-backtest.ts` run) mapping setup class (signalType×interval) → allowed by rule **PF ≥ 1.05 AND expectancy > 0.1 pts** from the ungated full-history backtest. Passes: fact-engine@1m/5m/15m + vector-side-entry@60m + any ≥3-counted-fact signal anywhere; BLOCKS all 1m/5m/15m ETH solo side-entries + 60m confluence. `runFactEngine`/`evaluateFormingBar` consult it as the FINAL firing filter (default ON; `qualityGateEnabled:false` escape hatch in the engine input, persisted key in market.tsx, no UI). **Gate sits BEFORE the cooldown-cursor update** — a blocked (invisible) signal must never suppress a later allowed one (regression-tested with a control fixture). Classes with NO data (zone-reaction, yellowbox-break) are NEVER blocked — "no data ≠ bad data"; blocking them would dead-end user rule 5 + the YELLOWBOX_SOLO setting at their first real signal.
- **Chicken-and-egg pattern for generated configs consumed by the module you're regenerating them FROM:** the harness computes gate+exits IN-MEMORY and passes them via a new `gateData` input override (`input.gateData ?? QUALITY_GATE`); the file write is only for the LIVE engine's next import. Never rely on re-importing a file you just rewrote in-process.
- **FEEDBACK-LOOP DEFECT (caught on the second run):** the harness's UNGATED baseline pass omitted `gateData` → the engine fell back to the IMPORTED quality-gate.ts, i.e. the PREVIOUS run's calibrated exits — so run 2's baseline PF/expectancy were measured under run 1's exits and fact-engine@60m silently flipped to ALLOWED (PF 0.96→1.33). Each run's gate would have depended on the previous run's checked-in file (non-idempotent from a fresh checkout). FIX + RULE: every enginePass takes a REQUIRED explicit `gateData`; the baseline uses a NEUTRAL config (gate off, `exitByClass:{}` = provisional 10/5). Any engine input that defaults to a generated file must be passed EXPLICITLY in a harness that regenerates that file.
- **MC exit calibration (user-dictated rules):** per class, SL = p85 MAE of TP1-winners (MAE measured BEFORE the TP touch), TP1 = expectancy-max grid 4..30 (0.25 steps, candidates need ≥30 winners), TP2 = 2×TP1. Implementation: per-signal excursion STEP FUNCTIONS (new-high-water [level, 1m-bar-ordinal] pairs for MFE and MAE, full horizon entry→17:00 settle, exit-config-independent) make every (TP,SL) simulation an O(steps) lookup with exact SL-first same-bar ties — no bar re-walking per grid candidate. Pooling: own class if n≥100 → same-interval gated pool if ≥100 → own class if n≥30 (cross-interval pooling mixes excursion scales; a 60m trade breathes ~10× a 1m trade) → all gated. Bootstrap 1,000× on the class's own signals → 95% CI. RESULT: fact-engine classes calibrate to WIDE exits (TP1 28–29.5/SL 13.5–14.25 — near the top of the user's 4..30 grid, flagged in the workbook: cap is a house rule, optimum may lie beyond); vse@60m tight (6.5/7.75). Per-class values live in the generated file's `exitByClass`; `resolveClassExit` in the engine applies them with precedence caller-explicit `input.exit` > class calibration > provisional 10/5, anchors (zone>yellowbox>tabletop) untouched.
- **HEADLINE:** 70,818 ungated → **7,731 gated (10.9%)**; expectancy 0.08 → **0.63 pts/trade**, PF 1.03 → **1.13**; per class fe@5m 0.35→0.99 (PF 1.12→1.21), fe@15m 0.57→1.17 (1.21→1.25), vse@60m 1.56→2.00 (win% 68.4). 30-day 5m tail 72 → 25 (all 47 ETH solos gone). Solo sheets confirm: solo yellowbox PF 0.89 (matches the earlier −EV finding), solo vector 1.02, solo footprint 1.49 (n=123, Jun–Jul 2026 window only).
- **Tests 78 → 97** (`npx tsx shared/fact-engine.test.ts`): mechanics fixtures that fire now-blocked classes take `qualityGateEnabled:false` (so a 0 means the RULE, not the gate — B6's secondary-ETH test would otherwise false-pass); fixtures asserting exact exit geometry PIN `exit:{DEFAULT_TP1_PTS:10,DEFAULT_SL_PTS:5}` (regen-proof vs future recalibration); gate tests use SYNTHETIC `gateData`, plus one consistency test over the real generated file (allowed ⇔ rule(pf,expectancy)) that survives regeneration.

**2026-07-14 — Yellow Box session-snap ported to CandlestickChart (the flagged separate task; /classic market chart)**
- The entry below flagged this port and warned the series "contains whitespace gap spacers" — STALE: gap spacers were removed long ago (see "Real unix timestamps (NO gap spacers)" pattern). The REAL index-shift hazard in CandlestickChart is the setData loop's invalid-bar FILTER (non-finite/≤0 OHLC, high≤low skipped) → `sortedCandlesRef` index ≠ series logical index. Fix: new `seriesTimesRef` mirroring the EXACT array passed to `series.setData()` — assigned right before `setData` (`candleData.map(d=>d.time)`) AND pushed in `updateLastBarClose`'s new-bucket branch (inside the same try as `series.update`) so the live-bar append stays 1:1 (verified live: 19663→19664 with the forming bar).
- Render block (drawAll): binary-search `lbTime(fromTime)` / `lbTime(toTime+1)−1` over `seriesTimesRef` → first/last bar indices; map index→x with `idxToX(i)=refBarX+(i−refBarI)·pxPerBar` anchored on the two RIGHTMOST bars where `ts.timeToCoordinate()` is non-null (v5 returns real coords — even negative — for any EXISTING bar time; null only for absent times, e.g. session-boundary stamps inside the 17:00-18:00 halt, which is exactly where the old `estX` fell into pxPerSec extrapolation and added ~3,500 phantom px per weekend). Deliberately NOT the `getVisibleLogicalRange()/cw` linear map used on the terminal: drawAll's `cw` includes the ~60px price axis, and the anchor form stays consistent with every other `timeToCoordinate` use in the file. `pxPerBar>0` gate (<2 visible bars → skip frame), `iLast<iFirst` skip (holiday/out-of-range), off-screen guard kept verbatim.
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` 0, `npm run build` 0; visually on /classic (the ONLY route where CandlestickChart is visible — MarketPage is display:none elsewhere): boxes on EVERY session, adjacent boxes tile edge-to-edge at session seams (each box's rawRight == next box's rawLeft: −8057/−6449/−4859/−3227/−1625/−17/1057), weekend = zero width, YB label + init-res/init-sup dashed lines intact. No series added/removed, no autoscale change.
- GOTCHA (hidden-tab verification harness): the Claude browser pane can be `document.hidden` → rAF never fires → lw-charts never paints (canvases stay 300×150) and `screenshot` times out. Workaround: patch `requestAnimationFrame = cb => setTimeout(cb,16)`, pull `chartRef`/`drawAllRef`/data refs off the React fiber (walk `el.__reactFiber$…` `.return`, scan hook `memoizedState` chain for `{current}` objects), use `chart.takeScreenshot()` (synchronous render) + call `drawAllRef.current()` directly, composite, export via `toDataURL`. CRITICAL: `setVisibleLogicalRange` is DEFERRED until a flush — `takeScreenshot()` once to flush BEFORE `drawAll()`, else the overlay renders one range behind the candles and looks like a coordinate bug that isn't.

**2026-07-14 (latest) — Every-session boxes fixed (bar-index X snapping) + FULL Milk zone stack served & drawn on the terminal**
- **DEFECT 1 (user: "boxes on only two sessions") — ROOT CAUSE: linear-TIME X extrapolation on a BAR-INDEX axis.** The overlay's `estX` fallback (`refX + (t−refT)·pxPerSec`) assumes the x-axis is linear in time, but lightweight-charts spaces by bar index — overnight/weekend gaps occupy NO width — so sessions scrolled off the visible range were over-stretched and pushed left until `rawRight<0` skipped them; only the ~2 nearest sessions survived (browser diagnosis: 2 distinct gold column-spans, one 520px wide). FIX: snap each session's edges to its FIRST/LAST candle indices (binary search over the ascending candle array) and map logical→x LINEARLY from `ts.getVisibleLogicalRange()`: `x = ((L − vr.from)/(vr.to − vr.from))·cw` — exact in bar-index space. **GOTCHA: do NOT use `ts.logicalToCoordinate()` — this lw-charts build returns 0 for every input here (not null!), silently collapsing every box to zero width with no exception; the failure is invisible without logging the raw values.** Precondition for index-snapping: the series data must equal the candle array (terminal: `setData(dedupe(candles))`, useTerminalData already dedup+sorts — holds). The market-page CandlestickChart has the SAME time-linear defect but its series contains whitespace gap spacers (logical≠array index) — flagged as a separate task, do not blind-port.
- Sessions with no candles (holidays / beyond loaded data) now naturally skip (no span), and boxes verified at multiple zoom levels + scroll-back: default view 6 distinct box segments covering 934/937 columns (was 2), zoomed 7/937, after deep scroll 6 with full coverage.
- **FULL ZONE SET end-to-end:** `shared/yellowbox-core.ts` gained LA_PCT_UP=0.68/SA_PCT_DN=0.50 (+ longAve/shortAve on CoreZones) AND the relocated persistent-band builder SPLIT for endpoint performance: `buildDayProfile(key,bars)` (per-day volume profile + birth candidates — the expensive part, cached per UNIQUE day) + `runBandLifecycle(profiles)` (cheap chronological merge/death pass) — `runBandLifecycle(last-45-profiles)` ≡ the old `buildPersistentBands(asOfKey)`, and `parseMwmlDoc` (pure doc→bands; navy 0,0,240=objectives, red 255,0,0=positioning, green 0,255,0=NFV, steel 40,85,125=pivot). scripts/yellowbox.ts refactored to import all of it — **verified byte-identical: full mwml render before/after diff shows ONLY the generatedAt timestamp**. (When removing local consts in favor of imports, grep for EVERY use first — PB_WIN etc. appeared again at line ~1481 in the JSON meta.)
- **Endpoint v2 (`server/yellowbox.ts`):** payload adds longAve/shortAve/normalRangeUp + `bands[]` ({type,label,top,bottom,color,source}) per day = mwml registry seeds (data/reference/*.mwml read once at module level, per-figure draw-start < D 09:30 ET cutoff, freshest-set-per-session-day dedup, side-relabeled vs last pre-open 5m close: objectives above=BUYER/below=SELLER, positioning above=SELLER/below=BUYER) + auto persistent bands filling uncovered price areas (3 above + 3 below: SELLER POSITIONING/BUYER OBJECTIVE/BUYERS ULTIMATE up, SELLERS OBJECTIVE/SELLERS ULTIMATE/BUYER POSITIONING down). **CACHE VERSIONING pattern: `ver INTEGER` column + YB_CACHE_VER const — rows with ver≠current are treated as uncached and lazily regenerate; new columns added via try/catch `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` (SQLite has no IF NOT EXISTS for columns).** Verified: Jul-13 serves its exact 8 mwml figures ([m]) + 6 auto ([a]); Jul-14 (no mwml) serves 6 auto.
- **Terminal render (draw-order per spec):** the day-zone stack draws FIRST (red initRes→maxRangeUp / green initSup→maxRangeDn shading at 0.07 alpha → dashed normalRange (grey)/maxTrend (white)/init (red/green tint) lines → cyan LongAve/ShortAve → gold YB box → labeled persistent bands at 0.10 fill with `LABEL bottom-top` text, skipped <11px tall or <90px wide) — all BEFORE the milk-zone block so milk zones/footprint/signals stay on top. Whole stack gated by the one YellowBox toggle: OFF wipes the canvas to 0 non-transparent px, ON restores.

**2026-07-14 — YELLOW BOX shipped as a platform strategy: live day-zone endpoint + gold boxes on the chart + a `yellowbox` fact in the engine**
- MISSION (user-ordered): implement the Yellow Box as a strategy AND draw every trading day's box on the chart when the toggle is on. Reused the two single-source assets end-to-end: `yellowbox-core` (calibrated walk-forward derivation) and `shared/fact-engine.ts`.
- **SHARED-CORE RELOCATION (single source of truth):** moved `scripts/yellowbox-core.ts` → `shared/yellowbox-core.ts` (pure, no better-sqlite3/DOM) and left `scripts/yellowbox-core.ts` as a one-line `export * from "../shared/yellowbox-core"` shim so the two scripts keep their `./yellowbox-core` import unchanged. WHY move: tsconfig `include` is `client/src`+`shared`+`server` only — `scripts/` is NOT type-checked and importing it from `server/` would drag `scripts/` into the tsc project + server bundle. `@shared/*` is the server's import convention (esbuild auto-reads tsconfig `paths`, so `@shared/yellowbox-core` bundles fine). VERIFIED the relocation is byte-identical: `npx tsx scripts/yellowbox.ts --date 2026-07-13` still prints YB `7616.50-7634.50` (settle 7625.75, prior day 2026-07-10) — the winning `settle+-mean60mExt50` value, maxAbs 0.50 vs Milk. Added one helper to the core: `priorCalendarDay(key)`.
- **PART 1 — endpoint `GET /api/yellowbox/day-zones?symbol&fromTs&toTs`** (`server/yellowbox.ts`, wired in `routes.ts` inside `registerRoutes`, DB access via `db.$client.prepare(...)`). Per-trading-day walk-forward: for each session-day K, settle = the most-recent trading day < K's close; box/initRes/initSup/etc = `computeCoreZones(last50, day60, settle)` — IDENTICAL math to the renderer/backtest (only uses days strictly before K, so cutoff 09:30-vs-18:00 is irrelevant to the values). Session span = [prior-CALENDAR-day 18:00 ET → K 17:00 ET] (Globex reopen→close). CACHE: `yellowbox_day_zones` table (`db.ts`, idempotent `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`, PK symbol+day_key) — completed days (sessionEnd < now) are immutable and frozen there; **holidays get a `traded=0` marker row** so a weekday with no bars is never re-scanned; today's/forming day is recomputed every request (never cached) until its session ends. Cold path loads 5m+60m only for `[minUncomputedStart − 130d … cap]` (never scans 1m); 8-year span guard. VERIFIED live: 2026-07-13 → box 7616.51-7634.53 settle 7625.75; 10 trading days in range, ZERO holiday false-boxes (July 4 2026 is a Saturday); each day matches Milk (e.g. 07-10 box 7577.01-7595.04 vs Milk 7577.00-7595.00).
- **PART 2 — chart layer (`CandlestickChart.tsx`), same mechanism as milk zones = the single `<canvas>` overlay in `drawAll`.** NO new lightweight-charts series (so the locked-in Y-autoscale is untouched — this was the whole point). Added a `yellowBoxes?: YellowBox[]` prop + `yellowBoxesRef` + a sync-and-`requestAnimationFrame(drawAll)` effect mirroring the `zones` one, and a render block right after the zone block (unconditional — NOT inside `if (zonesRef.current.length>0)`, which would never run when no milk zones are uploaded). Reuses `estX()` for the session-span X and `series.priceToCoordinate()` for Y; gold box `rgba(245,217,10,·)` (#f5d90a) + thin dashed init-res `rgba(239,154,154)` / init-sup `rgba(165,214,167)` lines (`ctx.setLineDash([5,3])`). market.tsx: a `useQuery` keyed `[symbol,fromTs,toTs]` (refetches on window-extend), mapped to `activeYellowBoxes` (chart) + `feDayZones` (engine); prop `yellowBoxes={yellowBoxEnabled ? activeYellowBoxes : []}`. Two persisted toggles in `mwb_settings`: `yellowBoxEnabled` (DEFAULT ON) + `yellowBoxSolo` (DEFAULT OFF) with a Settings "Yellow Box Strategy" section.
- **CRITICAL VISUAL BUG found only via a browser screenshot (pixel scans alone missed it):** off-screen boxes must be SKIPPED, not drawn full-width. My first cut used `bxRight = rawRight>=0 ? min(cw,rawRight) : cw` — for a box whose Globex session is entirely LEFT of the visible window, `estX` extrapolates a negative coordinate, so `rawRight<0` hit the `: cw` fallback and every one of the ~75 historical days tiled the pane as a full-width gold stripe (gold pixels 1.65M = 64% of canvas). FIX: `if (rawRight < 0 || rawLeft > cw) continue;` then clip `bxLeft=max(0,rawLeft)`, `bxRight=min(cw,rawRight)` — gold dropped to a clean 0.05% (discrete session-width boxes). LESSON: a pixel-count "boxes are drawing" check is necessary but NOT sufficient — LOOK at the render; a coordinate fallback that "keeps it on screen" can silently flood the chart.
- **PART 3 — `yellowbox` fact in `shared/fact-engine.ts` (additive; all 54 prior tests stayed green, 9 new = 63/63).** New optional `dayZones?: YellowboxDayZone[]` engine input (plain data; market.tsx adapter supplies it). On an RTH confirmation-candle CLOSE outside the day's box: `close>boxTop`→bullish, `close<boxBottom`→bearish, label `Yellowbox(break↑ @<boxEdge>)`. PRIMARY break = driver (weight `W_YELLOWBOX=2`, comparable to a normal milk-zone fact); SECONDARY-interval breaks corroborate (never a driver, and `decide()`'s C9 strip now also drops opposing secondary yellowbox facts so they can never veto a primary side-entry). RTH-ONLY (never enumerated in ETH — stays inside the `if (rth)` block). TP anchoring: when a yellowbox fact fires, that day's initRes(long)/initSup(short) joins the `computeExit` anchor set with rank priority zone<yellowbox<tabletop. SOLO (`YELLOWBOX_SOLO`, default off — full-history solo box-breaks are −EV, PF 0.80-0.89): a lone primary break fires `signalType:'yellowbox-break'` with TP1=initRes/initSup, SL=opposite box edge.
- **BROWSER-VERIFY GOTCHA (this app):** the market `CandlestickChart` is inside a collapsible/`display:none` panel; on a fresh load it renders 0×0 and the visible chart is a SEPARATE floating `.tt-livechart` (TerminalLiveChart) that this task did NOT wire. Screenshots also render the app tiny in the top-left of the pane. To verify the real chart I read the `yellowBoxes` prop straight off the React fiber (`__reactFiber$…` → `memoizedProps.yellowBoxes` = 75 boxes with correct values), then force-rendered it (un-hide the `display:none` ancestor + give the flex chain an explicit height + reposition as a fixed overlay) and pixel-scanned the canvas. If Yellow Boxes are ever wanted on the `.tt-livechart` terminal too, that's a separate wiring job (out of this task's Part-2 scope, which named `CandlestickChart.tsx`).

**2026-07-13 (latest) — FACT-ENGINE HARDENING: all 22 adversarial-review defects fixed against the user's trading-rule spec; BE + trailer + ALL delta logic permanently deleted**
- CONTEXT: an independent adversarial review of the day-old fact engine confirmed 22 defects (A1–E22) against the user's dictated rule spec. This round fixed every one. Engine stays framework-agnostic; live (market.tsx), SignalsPanel standalone, backtest.tsx, and the background notification scanners now ALL run `runFactEngine` — there is no other decision path left anywhere.
- **ENGINE fixes (shared/fact-engine.ts):** (B5/B6) ETH enumerates ONLY primary-interval vector facts — secondaries/zones/footprint are RTH-only, so ETH fires solo `vector-side-entry` ONCE on the event bar (no flood, no type degradation). (B7) LOOKAHEAD guard: a secondary bar corroborates only once its close (`start + inferred barSec`) ≤ the primary bar's close; spacing is INFERRED from the data (`inferBarSec`), not the declared label, which also defuses D18-style mislabeled slices inside the engine. (C9) secondaries can never veto: in contradiction weighing, the side opposing a PRIMARY side-entry driver has its SECONDARY vector facts stripped; stalemates remain possible among non-vector facts/primary drivers. (C10/C11) `Fact.counted:false` for tabletop + heading — label-notes/TP-anchors only, excluded from the ≥2 tally and weights (RTH SE+tabletop alone no longer fires). (D13) cooldown is one GLOBAL cursor per interval (4 bars, both directions). (D14) strong-zone touches are EPISODES (rising-edge count; consecutive touching bars = 1; re-touch needs ≥1 bar fully away). (D15) walk-forward resolves SL FIRST when one bar spans stop+target (conservative). (D16) ALL session gates (RTH/ETH classify, break, 15:15) evaluate at bar CLOSE time (`c.time + barSec`) — a bar closing exactly 15:15 ET is suppressed. (D17) tabletop flatness tests max−min across ALL 3 segment bars, not endpoints.
- **C8 intra-candle zone reaction (market.tsx adapter):** the forming primary bar is evaluated live (RTH, pre-15:15, cooldown vs last engine fire, HOD/LOD suppression, room check) with `detectZoneReaction` on its current OHLC + real-footprint corroboration through the same `decide()`; strong solo or ≥2 counted facts fire immediately at the forming bar's time. CANCEL semantics are architectural: the memo recomputes on every live bar update, so a failed reaction vanishes before the 3s auto-trade liveness re-check; on close the engine re-fires the same `${time}_${direction}` key, so locks transition seamlessly. Bar-close backtest path approximates with the same-bar wick+reaction test (documented in the engine header).

**2026-07-13 (later) — BIGGEST STRATEGY CHANGE EVER: retired the points system, built the logic-based FACT ENGINE (`shared/fact-engine.ts`), moved RTH close 4:30→5:00 PM ET everywhere**
- USER MANDATE (dictated + refined via Q&A): replace the entire signal decision model. GONE: close-vs-vector hard gate, vector slope gate, 60m-declining veto, ALL footprint delta logic (delta sign/divergence veto/delta-strength), the 4/3/1-pt scoring with totalPts>=4, the single "safe" tier, break-even, trailer. NEW model = enumerate directional FACTS and fire on logic (no points).
- **ARCHITECTURE (the load-bearing decision):** the whole decision model lives in a FRAMEWORK-AGNOSTIC module `shared/fact-engine.ts` (no React/DOM/lw-charts imports; plain functions over candle/zone/vector arrays). `market.tsx`'s `allConfluenceSignals` useMemo is now a THIN ADAPTER that marshals live state into the engine's plain-data inputs (`{primary, slices:[{interval,candles,vector}], zones, footprintByTime, settings, exit, nowSec}`) and maps `FactSignal[]`→`CSig[]`. WHY: the next task (Node regen + Monte-Carlo exit calibration over 2019→now) imports the SAME module, so live and backtest can never diverge. Reuses `shared/firing/{vector,session,types}.ts` (computeVectorLine, classifyZoneBullish, isRTH/isMarketBreak/isAfter315ET/rthSettleOfDay).
- **THE MODEL as built:** VECTOR — side entry = shelf (consecutive bars whose bodies overlap within a small band, hugging the vector) then a vector cross. LONG fires on the breakout candle itself; SHORT needs the down-cross + ≥2 consecutive bearish lower closes. SAME rules run on all 4 intervals; secondaries corroborate (never veto/modify primary). Tabletops (flat vector plateau, |vec−vec₋₂|≤0.5) are level facts → anchor TP1 just short of them. ZONE — reaction firing: wick touches zone + close moves ≥ N (ZONE_REACTION_PTS, default 2.0, settings-exposed); STRONG reaction (≥2×N move-away OR ≥2 touches) is the ONLY solo fact (RTH). FOOTPRINT — imbalance ZONES (kept the existing cluster detection) as support(long)/resistance(short); corroborating only, never solo, RTH+ETH. CONFLUENCE — fire when: solo strong-zone (RTH), OR ETH solo vector side-entry, OR ≥2 agreeing facts with ≥1 real DRIVER; ROOM check (opposing obstacle nearer than TP1 blocks); heavy-contradiction stalemate (|weightL−weightS|<margin ⇒ fire nothing); never opposing signals on one candle. LABEL — every signal lists its facts, e.g. `Vector(5m SE↑ + 15m tabletop) + Zone(support @6512)`; NEVER "confluence".
- **KEY DESIGN CATCH found by unit tests:** "heading toward side entry" must be a NON-driver (corroboration only). Initially made it a driver → a flat consolidation (heading + tabletop on one interval = 2 facts) fired spuriously. Fix: DRIVERS = fired side-entry / zone reaction only; heading + tabletop + footprint are corroboration. The user's own 3-fact example ("5m heading + 15m FIRED + 1m tabletop") still fires because the 15m FIRED side-entry is the driver.
- **EXITS provisional** (`EXIT_CALIBRATION` config object the MC step overwrites): TP1 10pt / SL 5pt / TP2 2×TP1 placeholders; TP1 anchors to nearest milk-zone edge or tabletop within reach (buffer), else default. Walk-forward TP2→TP1→SL, force-close at the NEW 5:00 PM ET settle, open-after-settle = loss.
- **SESSION-TIME ALIGNMENT (RTH 9:30–17:00 ET, break 17:00–18:00, DST-safe):** fixed every 4:30/4:00 site. `isMarketBreak` 16:30→17:00 in market.tsx + shared/firing/session.ts + SignalsPanel.tsx. `rthSettleOfDay` 4:30→5:00 and made DST-safe (Intl etWall helper) in market.tsx + trading-utils.ts + shared/firing/session.ts (backtest.tsx kept UTC-consistent 21:00). detectMilkZones close cap 4:30→5:00 in market.tsx + trading-utils.ts + backtest.tsx. footprint-analysis getCurrentSessionType/getLastCompletedSession RTH end 4:30→5:00 (ETH span 17h→16.5h). milkZones.ts uploaded-zone window 4:00→5:00. Legacy bg scanners' `utcH>=20` (4pm) break-skips → `isMarketBreak`. routes.ts isRTH/isMarketClosed + Clock.tsx were ALREADY 9:30–17:00 (no change). NOT touched: gap-audit.ts (CT-based, already 5–6pm break), DayTrading/ (hard constraint).
- **DB label (additive):** `label TEXT` on signal_history — schema.ts + idempotent `ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN label TEXT` in db.ts + routes.ts POST (body type + values + `excluded.label` in onConflictDoUpdate.set so the resolved-pass write keeps it). Rendered instead of tier badges: terminal SignalsView STRATEGY column (via useTerminalData `mapSignal`, falling back to the previously-DEAD `strategyLabel.ts` for old label-less rows) + SignalsPanel row.
- **VERIFICATION (real numbers):** `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0 after every edit + final; `npm run build` EXIT 0. 17/17 offline unit tests (side-entry long on breakout; short needs 2 bearish closes; solo strong-zone fires; single weak fact doesn't; 2 agreeing facts fire; room-check blocks obstacle<MIN_TP1; contradiction stalemate → null; 4-bar cooldown; ETH side-entry solo; labels contain every fact). 30-day REAL DB run (MES 5m primary + 1m/15m/60m, proxy footprint, no uploaded zones): 178 signals over 5,742 candles = **3.10% fire rate** (sane band 0–10%), Long 153/Short 25, RTH 24/ETH 154, **0 opposing signals on any candle**, labels list every fact. Long/ETH skew is expected (no uploaded zones ⇒ RTH needs multi-interval confluence while ETH fires solo side-entries; longs fire on the breakout candle, shorts need 2 confirms). App hot-loaded on :3000 (17 canvases, no console errors).

The client is already hot-loaded and sends `label`; the OLD server harmlessly ignores the extra body field (no breakage). Do NOT restart the preview server yourself (preview-managed, hard constraint).

**2026-07-09 (later) — MW-RECONCILIATION: phantom-delete in the backfill path + always-closed direct cleanup + MW-covered filter gating (supersedes never-delete for MW reconciliation)**
- USER MANDATE (explicit, supersedes the old "never delete" convention FOR MW-RECONCILIATION ONLY): remove the "floating false candles" (closed-period phantoms) and the "same wicks at multiple candles that are false" (low-vol displaced single-print bars) throughout ALL history. MW's getBars is authoritative, so a timestamp inside a range MW answered for which MW has NO bar cannot be real.
- ROOT CAUSE of BUG-1 (floating candles / false-wick clusters): the v2 backfill OVERWRITES timestamps MW returns (onConflictDoUpdate) but NEVER REMOVES our rows at timestamps MW has no bar. Verified in the July-3→5 closure window: 12–13 phantom rows each on 1m/5m/60m (e.g. `5m 2026-07-04T01:00Z O7555.75 V244` — a Saturday bar). Also 279 low-vol (V≤5) displaced non-flat 5m prints in the last 90d alone (e.g. `5m 2026-07-03T16:15Z close 7624.25 V2` vs real ~7555) — the "identical false wicks." The mid-session displaced prints at real timestamps get HEALED (overwritten) by reconciliation, not deleted (MW has a real bar there).
- FIX 1 — RECONCILE-DELETE in the backfill path (`server/gap-audit.ts`): added `recordBackfillBars(id, timestamps)` (fed from `live-bars.ts` on every v2 `bulk_bars` batch, recording the RAW returned timestamps BEFORE validation so a real MW bar our validator rejects can never be treated as a phantom) + `reconcileRange()` called in `onBackfillDone`. **SAFETY RULE (why it can't wipe real data):** reconcile ONLY when `count>0` (MW returned real bars → chart is active), and ONLY within `[min(returned)..max(returned)]` (the span MW ACTUALLY covered) — NEVER the raw requested range. This defends against LiveBarRelay `serviceBackfill` step-2 `source:"chart"` degrade (when getBars returns nothing it falls back to the chart-LOADED DataSeries slice, which may cover only PART of the range) — clamping to the returned span means we never delete real DB bars outside what MW could see. `count===0` reconciles NOTHING (an idle chart that can't answer is indistinguishable from a real closure). Deep `[0..cap]` probe never reconciled. Every delete logged (`[gap-audit] RECONCILE SYM:RES deleted N phantom rows within MW-returned span…`). 4/4 offline safety unit tests pass (phantom deleted + reals kept; partial-span keeps outside rows; count=0 no-op; deep no-op).
- FIX 1b — FULL-HISTORY RESYNC (`gap-audit.ts` `requestFullResync`/`enqueueFullResync`, `POST /api/data/full-resync/:sym/:res`): the normal `auditGaps` requests only MISSING session-open runs, so it SKIPS OVER closed-period phantoms (they sit at `!isSessionOpen` grid points between real bars, never "missing"). The force-resync instead sweeps the WHOLE `[provider-earliest..now]` range in reconcile-enabled MAX_RANGE_BARS chunks — each chunk MW answers (count>0) reconcile-deletes the phantoms MW omits INSIDE it, including closed-session floating candles interior to a chunk. Endpoint also clears retryable `no_data` first so wrongly-marked holes get re-requested.
- FIX 2 — ALWAYS-CLOSED DIRECT CLEANUP (`POST /api/data/purge-closed`, also run once directly): rows at times `isMarketClosed()` says the market is ALWAYS shut (Sat; Fri>17ET; Sun<18ET; the 17–18ET daily halt) can be deleted outright across ALL history — no real bar can exist there, independent of MW retention. Ran it: **deleted 821 always-closed rows** (MES 1m=402, 5m=349, 15m=5, 60m=57; ES 1m=1, 5m=1, 60m=6) → re-count = 0 across every SYM:RES. This alone removed all 36 phantom rows in the July-3→5 closure window; the only 4 rows left there are the LEGITIMATE Sunday-18:00-ET reopen (`2026-07-05T22:00Z`, 5m V4212 / 60m V22141). Do NOT hardcode a holiday calendar — early-close phantoms OUTSIDE always-closed windows are MW-reconciliation's job.
- FIX 3 — MW-COVERED FILTER GATING (`server/routes.ts` cached-continuous, `serveFilter()` + `mwCoverageTs()`): for bars newer than a resolution's `sync_state.earliest_ts` (MW-authoritative, ingest-validated, reconcile-cleaned) BYPASS the heavy heuristic trio (`dropWickSpikes`/`dropIsolatedSpikes`/`dropCompletedGhostBars`) — keep only the cheap malformed-guard (`isSpikeBar`) + `isMarketClosed` + flat + off-grid guards. Older-than-coverage bars keep the FULL chain (old untrusted pipeline). No `sync_state` for a res ⇒ coverTs=Infinity ⇒ everything keeps the full chain (safe default). RATIONALE: those heavy filters were built for the old stitched pipeline and would drop thin-but-real overnight MW bars (V5–50). "Serve every single MW candle." 2/2 gating split tests pass. **DEPENDENCY: because gating bypasses the trio, the 279 displaced phantom prints MUST be healed by the full-resync BEFORE this is safe to serve — otherwise they leak. Roll them out together (restart on this code THEN run full-resync).**
- BACKUP (verified before ANY delete): better-sqlite3 online `.backup()` (WAL-safe, live — NO need to stop the server, unlike VACUUM INTO) → scratchpad, integrity_check=ok, then copied to `data/app.db.backup-pre-mw-reconcile-2026-07-09` (1.0GB, integrity ok). Online `.backup()` checkpoints the WAL so its counts include committed WAL data (why it showed 1m=2.33M vs a naive readonly-open showing 200k — the readonly open without full WAL read undercounts a busy DB).
- OBSERVED (live DB, mid-session): since yesterday all FOUR MES resolutions handshook + deep-backfilled (1m 200k→2.33M back to 2019, 15m→2022, 60m→2020) — all now have `sync_state`, so gating + reconcile apply to all four. BUG-2 (1m holes): a 1040-min 1m gap Jul-8 19:57Z→Jul-9 13:17Z remains (deep history filled but the recent overnight not yet) — no `no_data` recorded for MES:1, so the force-resync (or next audit) will re-request it.
- BLOCKER (reported to user, NOT worked around): the live verification (task 4) + the MW-reconciliation full-resync + gating verification REQUIRE the server to run THIS new code. The user's PRE-EXISTING dev server (PID from session start) holds ports 3000/5000 and is actively backfilling; the auto-mode classifier BLOCKS killing a process this session didn't create, and 3000/5000 are occupied so a second instance can't bind. So: code + always-closed cleanup + backup are DONE and tsc-clean; the full-resync sweep + live 4a–4e verification are PENDING a server restart on this branch's code (user must stop their server, or authorize it).

**2026-07-09 — MotiveWave is now the SINGLE SOURCE OF TRUTH: ported the v2 server-driven getBars backfill protocol onto `restore/jul1-plus-v2-studies` + demoted the disk-ingest phantom factory**
- ARCHITECTURE CHANGE (user-approved): stop stitching 4 unreliable candle sources (MW `.bar_data1` disk files, `.tick_data` reconstruction, price-only ticks, Yahoo patches). Every glitch class this week (phantom bars, stale opens, splice corruption, frozen intervals) came from that stitching. Solution: the deployed v2 `LiveBarRelay.java` (already on this branch) sends a `hello` handshake `{type:"hello",symbol,resolution,seriesStartMs,seriesEndMs,ver:2}` per chart, then services server-requested `{type:"backfill",id,fromMs,toMs}` by streaming `bulk_bars` batches (500/msg, id/seq-tagged) + `backfill_done{count,earliestAvailableMs}`. Ported the SERVER side of that protocol from the SDK branch (`claude/historical-data-fetch-fix-j2sw91`, commit 50d82fd) onto the current July-1 lineage.
- PORTED: (1) `server/gap-audit.ts` VERBATIM (self-contained: CME Globex session grid, per-(SYM:RES) `StudySync` dispatcher, deep-history probe `{fromTs:0,toTs:earliestStored,deep:true}` on first-ever sync, one-in-flight backfill, `unfillable_ranges` provider_cap/no_data memory with retry-once so it never infinite-loops, hourly re-audit, `getCompleteness`). (2) `server/roll-heal.ts` VERBATIM (`detectAndHeal` — shifts older stored bars by a constant roll δ when the overlap disagrees uniformly by >½ ES tick). (3) `shared/schema.ts` + `server/db.ts`: 3 additive `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` (sync_state, unfillable_ranges, adjustment_log) — NO destructive SQL on the live 820MB DB. (4) `server/routes.ts`: read-only `/api/data/gaps/:sym/:res` (completeness) + `DELETE /api/data/unfillable/:sym/:res` (clears only retryable `no_data`, never `provider_cap`, never cached_candles).
- MERGE DECISION — `server/live-bars.ts` (the tricky part): MERGED the v2 protocol INTO the current file, did NOT overwrite it. KEPT ALL current-branch functionality the SDK version lacked: port-5000 bridge server (studies connect to :5000), `reconnectMWStudies`, `setTickRelayConnected`, AutoTrader order-commands sockets + socketAlive ping/pong, `footprint_bar`, `cacheInvalidate`, preloaded `_fpAddBar`, MW ping/pong, `lastDumpMaxTs` re-dump guard, `validateBar`/`isSaneBarTime`/float32-bound/body-anomaly chain in persistBar/persistBulk. ADDED: `studySockets` Map (SYM:RES→ws), `hello`/`backfill_done` handlers → `onStudyConnected`/`onBackfillDone`, `registeredKeys` per-socket + close-handler unregister → `onStudyDisconnected`, v2 bulk_bars `id`/`seq`/`bulk_report` ack + `detectAndHeal` before persist.
- KEY MERGE INSIGHT (the one that makes healing work): the current branch's re-dump high-water guard (`newBars = cleanBars.filter(b => b.t > watermark)`) DROPS bars OLDER than what the DB already has — correct for v1 constant re-dumps, but it would silently discard EVERY v2 backfill bar (gap-audit requests exactly the ranges that are MISSING/old). So I gated BOTH the fast re-dump rejection AND the watermark filter behind `if (!isV2)` (isV2 = `id` present). v2 bars bypass the guard entirely and overwrite via `onConflictDoUpdate` — that IS the mechanism that heals history to MW-identical values. v2 bars still flow through the full validation chain (validateBar rejects off-grid/ghost/spike; float32 bounds; body-anomaly) — getBars is authoritative but a Java reflection bug could still emit garbage, so guards stay.

**2026-07-08 (later) — "No charts are accurate": FOUR distinct causes found and fixed (commit d310419 + Yahoo splice repair)**
- USER REPORT after the DB restore + July-2 port: "no charts are accurate at all." Systematic diagnosis found FOUR interacting causes — none of which were the filters themselves (today's 5m was verified 99% accurate vs Yahoo, 132/134 closes within 1pt):
- CAUSE 1 — 1m chart 5.5h STALE: raw DB had no 1m rows after 14:02Z despite live ticks flowing (live in-progress bar current, but never PERSISTED). Root cause: tick-built in-progress bars carry `volume: 0` (TickRelay ticks are price-only), and the July-2 `validateBar` ghost-guard in `bulkUpsert` silently rejected every completed tick-built bar. THE LESSON: a write-guard added later can silently kill a legitimate producer whose data shape predates the guard — when adding write-validation, grep for every producer of that data and check each one's field population. FIX: count ticks as volume (`volume += 1` per tick, seed 1 — same convention as the tick-file parser); real MW volume still overwrites via onConflictDoUpdate.
- CAUSE 2 — PHANTOM-OPEN bars from stale boot seeds: at boot, in-progress bars are seeded from the last tick-FILE price (hours stale when MW's tick files lag, e.g. 7559.25 vs market 7525). First real tick only updated close/low → bar persisted with phantom open/high 35pt off. Worse, CAUSE-1's fix made these persistable (volume now > 0). FIX: `volume === 0` marks a never-ticked boot seed — RESEED the whole bar (OHLC=price) on the first real tick instead of updating. Applied to 1m/5m/60m in BOTH notifyExternalTick and the disk-file tick path.
- CAUSE 3 — the Jul-2→Jul-5 SPLICE region (merged fresh-SDK rows) contained phantom-OPEN bars (O 7590-7615 vs real 7525-7550, e.g. 5m 07-02T22:55 O7601.5 L7535 V143) that evade ALL filters because only open/high are corrupt while the CLOSE is real — clustering uses closes (passes), lone-wick needs body-inside (body spans the phantom open → fails), volume 143-281 above the 100 floor. NEW GLITCH SHAPE to remember: "stale-open" — huge upper wick + body from a false open, real close. FIX: Yahoo overwrite where `max(|dO|,|dH|,|dL|,|dC|) > 4pt` for Jul-2 12:00→now (5m: 77 repaired/911 ok; 15m: 1; 60m: 31; 1m: 103 + 705 missing inserted), then 60m REBUILT from the now-verified 5m aggregation for the window (22 more; all flagged 60m bars now dev 0.0 from 5m agg). Two last-hour phantom rows (written before the code fix loaded; Yahoo didn't have those minutes yet) patched from neighboring closes.
- CAUSE 4 — false positives in MY OWN spike scan: range>25pt is NORMAL for a 60m bar (V133k-144k real hours) and the 15m 08:15 V30207 was a real 40pt news drop — verify against Yahoo/5m-agg before calling an hourly bar a glitch; don't reuse the 1m/5m thresholds on higher timeframes.
- VERIFIED END-TO-END: post-fix, 1m/5m have ZERO range>25pt anomalies over 6 days; live 1m persistence confirmed by watching a fresh bar land in the DB 1 minute after its bucket closed (Monitor until-loop); page renders (17 canvases, no errors; preview screenshots may time out on the ~176k-bar initial render — use preview_eval readyState/canvas-count instead). Backup at `data/app.db.backup-pre-splice-repair-2026-07-08` (VACUUM INTO scratchpad first — faster off OneDrive — then copied to data/).

**2026-07-01 — GLITCH CANDLES still rendering on the BAXTER chart → root cause was a FILTER GAP (low-volume wick/body spikes); added `dropWickSpikes` permanent fallback**
- SYMPTOM: user screenshots showed "thin vertical line" glitch candles STILL on the live 5m chart (and repeated across history/intervals), despite the prior Yahoo repair. My earlier "they're hidden by the read filters" claim was WRONG for these.
- ROOT CAUSE: a whole GLITCH FAMILY evaded every existing filter. The `cached-continuous` chain (server/routes.ts) filters flat(`h=l`), off-grid(`ts%resSec`), `isSpikeBar` (absolute range/close > 2.5–7%), `isMarketClosed`, and `dropIsolatedSpikes` (displaced BODY only). A bar with a LONE displaced HIGH or LOW (a 20–200pt wick/body spike, low volume) passes ALL of them: its total range is below the absolute `isSpikeBar` threshold (40pt ≪ 4% of 7500 = 300pt), and `dropIsolatedSpikes` only tests the body (low>both closes / high<both closes), not a single wick. e.g. `MES 5m 2026-06-08 12:15 H7540.5 body0.5 V19`, `2026-06-30 09:40 H7505 V0`, `2026-06-01 01:00 H7675 L7468 V29`.
- KEY (same as the repair session): VOLUME is the discriminator. True glitches are near-zero volume; real news/settlement moves are V400+. A neighbour-relative scan alone is CONTAMINATED with real bars (V48118 FOMC, V8408 gap, V43453) — must gate on volume.
- FIX (permanent fallback, non-destructive, honors never-delete): added `dropWickSpikes()` in `server/routes.ts` (right after `dropIsolatedSpikes`, ~line 231) and applied it in BOTH serving paths — the DB path (wraps the filter chain, BEFORE the 15m aggregation so a glitchy 5m wick can't corrupt aggregated 15m high/low) and `memBarsToCandles`. Logic: for bars with `volume < volMin(=100)`, drop if `high - max(adjNeighbourHighs) > tol` OR `min(adjNeighbourLows) - low > tol`, `tol = max(10pt, 0.12%*close)`, neighbours must be temporally adjacent (`≤ interval*3`), left-neighbours use last-KEPT bars, iterate up to 4 passes. Bars with `volume ≥ 100` are ALWAYS kept (real moves preserved).
- VERIFIED: tsc EXIT 0. Offline sim across all MES intervals: dropped 1m=2425 / 5m=619 / 15m=0 / 60m=31 glitches, and **maxVol among ALL dropped bars = 99/96/–/85 (< 100)** → ZERO real-bar collateral. Live endpoint after restart: the 4 circled glitch timestamps ABSENT, the 3 real high-vol bars PRESENT. Browser screenshot: chart renders clean, no vertical-line spikes.
- COVERAGE: one server-side filter covers EVERY consumer — web terminal, mobile app, and the market.tsx engine all fetch `/api/data/cached-continuous`, so all intervals/all symbols are sanitized at serve time regardless of what's in the DB. This is the durable "always-there fallback" the user asked for. `routes.ts` is NOT StrategyGuard-protected (guard only covers `strategies/*.json`), so no re-hash needed.
- STILL OPEN (optional): the underlying rows remain in the DB (never-delete). An active INGESTION leak is still WRITING fresh off-grid V0 15m bars (mw-reader/live-bars) — a write-time guard would be belt-and-suspenders but the read filter already guarantees they never render.

**2026-07-01 — Historical false-candle SURGICAL repair (MES, repair-only/never-delete) — 156 bars fixed from Yahoo**
- USER TASK: find & fix false/ghost/no-body candles across ALL historical data. USER DECISIONS (AskUserQuestion): (a) fix method = REPAIR ONLY, NEVER DELETE (overwrite reachable bars from Yahoo, leave older defects in place); (b) scope = MES only; (c) fix flat/off-grid/weekend auto but SHOW the ghost list first.
- CRITICAL FINDING — the ghost/spike detector (`dropIsolatedSpikes`, routes.ts:185) is CONTAMINATED with REAL bars when used as a flat "these are all false" list. It flagged genuine high-volume news bars (MES 5m 2025-10-12/13 cluster V1500–2100; 60m 2026-03-20 19:00 V361,655; 2026-04-20 15:00 V196,347) AND even FLAT `h=l` bars that are REAL quarterly triple-witching SETTLEMENT prints (60m 2024-09-20/2024-12-20/2025-06-20 20:00–21:00 UTC, V1.4M–2.1M). RULE: VOLUME is the reliable discriminator — true phantoms are LOW volume (V0–~75); real settlement/news bars are V800+. Only repair the low-vol subset (`volume<100`). NEVER treat the raw spike-filter output as a delete list (it's designed to run reversibly at read time on a rendered window).
- DB REALITY: prior cleanups left it mostly clean — malformed(h<l)=0, non-positive=0, ALL 1m clean. Remaining defects were modest: MES 5m/15m/60m flat 5/5/14, off-grid 5/5/17, + low-vol ghost phantoms (5m ~160 reachable, 60m ~17 reachable, 1m/15m ~0).
- MECHANISM: surgical Yahoo overwrite. `new YahooFinance({suppressNotices:['yahooSurvey']})` (v3.13.2 — must instantiate, NOT `yahooFinance.chart` directly like v2). Windows must stay JUST INSIDE Yahoo limits: 5m ≤ 59d (60d boundary is REJECTED as "must be within last 60 days"), 60m ≤ 725d. Fetched ES=F, matched by exact unix timestamp, overwrote OHLC only where Yahoo had a real (`high>low`) bar within ±5% of a DB neighbour close. LEFT VOLUME UNTOUCHED (ES-scale volume must not overwrite MES-scale rows). Targets = reachable && on-grid (`ts%sec===0`) && `volume<100` && (`h=l` OR ghost).
- RESULT: 183 targets → 156 repaired (5m 144/160, 60m 12/23); 16 left (Yahoo lacked the overnight bar), 6 skipped-unsafe (Yahoo also flat / failed ±5%), 5 no-op (already matched Yahoo = real bars the filter over-flagged). Verified: low-vol phantom ghosts 5m 160→16, 60m 17→5 (remainder = unreachable, still hidden by read filters). 0 malformed introduced. NOTHING deleted.
- OFF-GRID CAVEAT: under never-delete, off-grid bars (wrong timestamp) CANNOT be repaired in place — Yahoo overwrite writes to the ALIGNED timestamp, so the off-grid phantom row remains (harmless — read filters drop it). Flat counts (5m=5 off-grid, 60m=14) therefore unchanged; those are off-grid or Yahoo-also-flat.
- 1m + 15m (ran on user request): 0 repairable targets within Yahoo reach. 1m — only 4 reachable ghost bars, ALL real high-vol news (V2845–7046), and older 1m phantoms are beyond Yahoo's ~7d 1m limit (unrepairable). 15m — 0 ghosts; the only reachable false candles are 6 OFF-GRID V0 no-body bars (ts%900 = 503/521/790/535/525/572), ALL fresh 2026-06-30/07-01 → a LIVE ingestion path is still writing off-grid 15m V0 rows (like the flat-candle ingestion leak noted elsewhere; suspects mw-reader/live-bars 15m bucket write). Deletion is the ONLY fix for off-grid rows; USER CHOSE to LEAVE them (keep never-delete) — they're read-filtered/hidden so harmless. If ever revisited: delete `WHERE timestamp % resSec <> 0` per res, and trace the 15m off-grid writer.
- SAFETY: backed up first via `VACUUM INTO data/app.db.backup-false-candle-repair-2026-07-01` (818MB) BEFORE any write. Repair script ran against the LIVE db while the server held it open (WAL, single-writer — fine). Server continuous-cache won't reflect changes until restart, but since these bars are already read-filtered, the visible chart is unchanged either way. ESM scripts can't use NODE_PATH — must live inside the project dir (or use tsx) to resolve node_modules.

**2026-07-01 — 60m standardized on :00 top-of-hour (prior :30 note was WRONG about Yahoo)**
- ROOT CAUSE: 60m bars were stored at TWO conflicting alignments. Yahoo ES=F 60m is `:00` top-of-hour (EMPIRICALLY VERIFIED: `yahooFinance.chart(ES=F,60m)` returns 22:00/23:00/00:00… UTC, 590/591 at :00) — NOT :30. But a prior session set the MW pipeline (`agg60mBucket`, `get60mBucket`, `notifyExternalTick`) to `:30` on the mistaken belief (see the now-corrected note below) that "Yahoo + LiveBarRelay use :30." Result: Yahoo wrote 7,589 `:00` MES bars (the deep 2yr history) while MW wrote 1,043 `:30` bars, and the serving filter `timestamp % 3600 === 0` at `routes.ts:903` SILENTLY DROPPED every :30 bar → live 60m vanished on reload + 30-min live-edge misalignment.
- DECISION (user): standardize on `:00` — it's where the deep Yahoo history lives, it's the standard convention, and the serving filter already keeps it. Tradeoff accepted: a 60m bar spans 9:00–10:00 (straddles the 9:30 RTH open) rather than RTH-aligned.
- CHANGES: (1) `mw-reader.ts agg60mBucket()` → `Math.floor(t/3600)*3600`. (2) `mw-reader.ts` startup purge INVERTED — now deletes `:30` rows (`% 3600 === 1800`), keeps `:00` (was deleting :00!). (3) `client/src/lib/trading-utils.ts get60mBucket()` → `:00`. (4) `DayTrading/lib/candles.ts aggToInterval` OFFSET→0.
- DATA REPAIR: MES 60m `:00` (Yahoo) had stopped at 2025-11-03 — recent MES 60m lived ONLY in the :30 bars. After deleting :30, re-ran `POST /api/data/yahoo-backfill {symbol:MES}` (Yahoo has ES=F 60m through today) → MES 60m :00 now continuous 2024-07-11 .. 2026-07-01, 11,306 served, all :00, 0 flat, 0 off-grid. ES likewise reaches current.
- CLEANUP: `VACUUM INTO data/app.db.backup-60m-align-2026-07-01T09-06-33`, deleted 1,068 `:30` rows. NOTE: the still-running old-code server keeps writing :30 until RESTART; those transient :30 rows are read-filtered and purged by the inverted startup DELETE on next boot.
- CORRECTION to the 2026-06-?? "60m duplicate bars" entry further down: its claim "Yahoo Finance and LiveBarRelay produce bars at RTH-aligned :30 boundaries" is FALSE for Yahoo (Yahoo=:00). That entry's :30 fix was the source of this bug.

**2026-06-30 — Flat-candle INGESTION leak closed in the LiveBarRelay path (5m/15m/60m)**
- Last session's `h===l` fix was only at the SERVING/read layer + a one-time DB delete. Flat bars kept re-entering the DB because the LiveBarRelay write path never rejected them. Audit found 93 NEW flat rows since the cleanup: MES15=23, ES60=20, MES5=18, MES60=16, ES5=9, ES15=7 — and **res=1 had ZERO** (the tick path `bulkUpsert` in `mw-reader.ts:824` already rejects `high<=low`). That split (0 on 1m, all on 5/15/60) localized the leak precisely to LiveBarRelay.
- ROOT CAUSE: `server/live-bars.ts` `persistBar` (~515) and the `bulk_bars` validator (~344) used `high < low`, which PASSES `high === low` (flat/no-range candles). FIX: changed both to `high <= low`. Left the `type:"bar"` broadcast guard (~263) at `<` on purpose — a FORMING bar (`complete:false`) is legitimately flat the instant a new candle opens; only completed/persisted bars must be non-flat.
- WHY removing flat bars never breaks continuity (user's "next candle opens at prev body" rule): a flat bar has open==close==price, so deleting it leaves prev.close == next.open at the same price — neighbours stay connected. Proven safe.
- DB CLEANUP: `VACUUM INTO data/app.db.backup-flat-clean-2026-06-30T10-36-17`, then `DELETE FROM cached_candles WHERE high = low` (93 rows). tsc clean. 1m path needed no change.
- HISTORICAL DATA REALITY CHECK (Yahoo): probed `yahooFinance.chart(ES=F, since 2000)` per interval — 1m ERRORS ("Only 8 days of 1m granularity allowed", and even 35d fails; only ~last 7d works), 5m/15m capped 60d, 60m capped 730d, ONLY 1d/1wk/1mo reach 2000 (1d=6552 bars back to 2000-09-18). So "1m back to 2000" is IMPOSSIBLE from Yahoo and CANNOT be derived from monthly (aggregation is fine→coarse only). Deep intraday requires a paid vendor file (FirstRate Data ~2008, Kibot, Databento 2010+) imported via `/api/data/import-csv`. ES e-mini itself only began Sept 1997.

**2026-06-27 — Chart accuracy pass: all 4 intervals (1m/5m/15m/60m) verified clean first-to-last**
- AUDITED `cached_candles` for MES across res 1/5/15/60 over the full range. Defect types & counts (raw DB): isolated spikes (1m 4820, 5m 1885, 60m 788), market-closed bars (1m 377, 5m 324), zero-range no-body (5m 16, 15m 21, 60m 8), zero-range WITH volume (60m 7 — leaked!), off-grid (60m 940).
- KEY FINDING #1 — the only actual READ leak: zero-range filter was `!(h===l && !volume)`, so flat bars WITH volume (7 on 60m) rendered as no-body candles. FIX: drop ALL `h===l` (no real MES bar is perfectly flat). Applied in both the DB path and `memBarsToCandles`.
- KEY FINDING #2 — 60m "off-grid" 940 bars are NOT all artifacts: ~129 at :30 are real (half-day RTH bars like Black Friday 09:30–12:30 w/ 100k+ vol). But 806 at :30 are PHANTOMS (wrong-contract, ~50–190pt off, low vol, e.g. 04:30 @7625 vs real ~7477). The `%3600===0` off-grid filter correctly drops them; verified before deleting so as not to lose real history.
- KEY FINDING #3 — interleaved phantoms leaked through `dropIsolatedSpikes`: a wrong-contract feed bled in on ALTERNATE 1m bars (10:00 @6852 / 10:01 @6798 / 10:02 @6848, real ~6750, vol 2–110). The inner phantom is shielded because its NEXT bar is also a phantom (not isolated on that side in one pass). FIX: rewrote `dropIsolatedSpikes` to (a) ITERATE until stable (outer phantoms removed → inner becomes isolated next pass), (b) be TIME-GAP aware — only treat a price-displaced bar as a spike when neighbours are temporally adjacent (≤3 intervals); a lone overnight bar whose neighbours are hours away is normal drift, NOT a spike, and must be kept, (c) validate the FIRST/LAST bar of a served window so a phantom at a pagination boundary can't leak.
- DB CLEANUP: backed up to `data/app.db.backup-pre-candle-clean-2026-06-27T15-56-31`, then permanently deleted 874 UNAMBIGUOUS garbage rows (malformed + zero-range h==l + market-closed) across all symbols. Did NOT permanently delete off-grid/spikes (heuristic/edge — handled reversibly at read time).
- VERIFIED: simulated the exact serving chain AND hit the live `/api/data/cached-continuous/MES/{iv}` endpoint — 1m=182685, 5m=102172, 15m=85294, 60m=11329 candles, ZERO zeroRange/offGrid/marketClosed/malformed/spike/isolated-spike across every candle.
- Filters live in `server/routes.ts`: `isMarketClosed` (~170), `dropIsolatedSpikes` (~185, now iterative+time-gap), the DB serving chain (~872) and `memBarsToCandles` (~841). Server must be restarted to pick up changes (clears the continuous cache). Confidence: high (live-endpoint verified).

**2026-06-26 — AutoTrader missing in MW = duplicate jars in BACKUP SUBFOLDERS inside the Extensions scan path (+ corrects a prior misdiagnosis)**
- SYMPTOM: after a new-laptop migration, AutoTrader would not appear in MotiveWave. User confirmed it worked perfectly before (it was always `strategy=true`, added via **Add Study**).

PROOF: with MW running, those backup-folder jars were file-locked by `MotiveWave.exe` — confirming MW had them open.
- NOTE on the 2026-06-25 entry below: it was PARTLY right and PARTLY wrong. RIGHT: `strategy=true` keeps it out of the normal "Add Study"/"All Studies" list. WRONG/incomplete: it isn't under a "Strategies" menu either — with no `menu` attribute it's nowhere. The duplicate classes were a SEPARATE, real problem (fixed above). The actual add path is `menu="Custom"` → **Study → Custom** (see FIX 2 below). The build.bat "Add Study → Auto Trader" instruction text is wrong; ignore it.
- FIX: moved BOTH backup subfolders out to `C:\Users\Jackson\MW-Extensions-Backups\` (outside MW's scan path). Had to terminate a leftover `MotiveWave.exe` launcher (PID held jar locks even after the UI closed; the main JVM had already exited). Left the VS Code redhat.java `java.exe` language-server process ALONE (not MotiveWave). Verified: Extensions has exactly 4 top-level jars, `jar tf` shows zero duplicate `com/custom/*.class` across them.
- RULE: NEVER place jar backups inside `MotiveWave Extensions\` — MW recurses. Back up to a sibling dir. To finish jar moves, MW must be FULLY closed (watch for the lingering `MotiveWave.exe` launcher after the window closes — it keeps jars locked).
- SECOND ROOT CAUSE (the real blocker, found after de-dup): a `strategy=true` study is EXCLUDED from the "All Studies" dialog (Ctrl+T; its Type filter is only All/Overlay/Signal — no "Strategy"), AND our `@StudyHeader`s have NO `menu` attribute, so AutoTrader had NO entry point ANYWHERE. Plain studies w/o `menu` still appear in All Studies (that's how TickRelay/LiveBarRelay get added); a STRATEGY w/o `menu` appears nowhere. The MW SDK example studies (Delta MA, MA High Low, Wallaby…) show under **Study → Custom** because they declare `menu="Custom"`.
- FIX 2: added `menu = "Custom"` to AutoTrader's `@StudyHeader` (valid field — test-compiled EXIT=0 vs the v70 SDK w/ JDK 25). Rebuilt AutoTrader.jar 14189→14199 bytes, installed. AutoTrader now appears under **Study → Custom**, NOT "Add Study". (`menu` is the way to make any custom study/strategy discoverable in the Study menu.)
- EDITION NOTE: license is `ORDER_FLOW` (MW log: `License Edition: ORDER_FLOW`). MW marketing implies strategy backtest/optimize is Professional+, but the user confirms this exact Order Flow + Apex setup ran AutoTrader live before — so running a custom strategy is NOT edition-gated here; the missing `menu` entry point was the whole problem. Also in log: Rithmic `permission denied` for get_order_book on ES (L2 depth perm) — watch order-routing perms when it fires.

**2026-06-26 — "Bugged throughout" = interleaved phantom bars; de-spike filter + Yahoo overwrite; studies blocked; rebuild Step 1**

HARD LIMIT: Yahoo ES=F intraday is 60d for 5m/15m, ~720d for 60m — it CANNOT overwrite 5m older than 60d (92k of 103k MES 5m bars are beyond reach), which is exactly why the read-side `dropIsolatedSpikes` (works on ALL history, no source needed) is the real fix, not the overwrite.

**2026-06-26 — Interval data accuracy fixes (60m alignment + 15m fallback + spike thresholds)**
- ROOT CAUSE of 60m duplicate bars: `mw-reader.ts aggregate()` was using `Math.floor(timeSec / 3600) * 3600` (UTC :00 boundaries like 13:00, 14:00 UTC) while Yahoo Finance and LiveBarRelay produce bars at RTH-aligned :30 boundaries (13:30, 14:30 UTC = 9:30 ET open). These wrote as separate DB rows, producing two 60m bars per period on the chart. The client-side `trading-utils.ts` `get60mBucket()` already had the correct `Math.floor((ts - 1800) / 3600) * 3600 + 1800` formula — the server just wasn't matching it.
- FIX: added `agg60mBucket()` helper in `mw-reader.ts` mirroring `get60mBucket()`, applied in `aggregate()` for periodMin===60, in `applyTick()`, and in `notifyExternalTick()`. Added a one-time SQL `DELETE FROM cached_candles WHERE symbol=? AND resolution='60' AND (timestamp % 3600) = 0` in `loadMultiDir` to purge stale :00-UTC rows already in the DB. Yahoo/LiveBarRelay bars at :30 are unaffected (`1800 % 3600 = 1800 ≠ 0`).
- ROOT CAUSE of 15m fallback bad data: when native 15m bars were absent and `cached-continuous` fell back to resolution "5", it returned raw 5m bars for a 15m chart request. The client's `isAligned("15m")` kept ONLY bars at 15m boundaries (the first 5m sub-bar of each 15m period) — wrong OHLCV values. FIX: added server-side aggregation in routes.ts `cached-continuous`: when `interval === "15m" && usedRes === "5"`, bucket by `Math.floor(timestamp / 900) * 900` and merge OHLCV (first open, max high, min low, last close, sum volume). Applied to both the DB path and the memory fallback path.
- `isSpikeBar` thresholds were too tight (`1m=0.5%, 5m=1.0%, 15m=1.5%, 60m=2.5%`) — real news bars (flash crashes, FOMC moves) were filtered. Float32 corruptions are 10x+ off from real price (~512, 8192 instead of ~5800), so safe thresholds are much higher. Raised to `1m=2.5%, 5m=4.0%, 15m=5.0%, 60m=7.0%`. Removed the doji-spike check (`body/range < 0.05`) that was incorrectly filtering valid doji candles.
- Removed redundant per-wick filter from `bulkUpsert` in `mw-reader.ts` (lines that checked `lowerWick/close > maxWickPct`). The existing 5% range check and the doji-wick body-ratio check already catch float32 corruptions; the wick check was mathematically dominated and only removed valid extreme-wick bars (pin bars, hammers, shooting stars).
- RULE: 60m bucket must ALWAYS use the RTH :30 offset (`Math.floor((t - 1800) / 3600) * 3600 + 1800`) on BOTH client and server. Any new server code touching 60m timestamps must use `agg60mBucket()` not `Math.floor(t / 3600) * 3600`.
- `tsc` clean. Confidence: high.

**2026-06-02 — Restored per-session footprint ladders + zones in the terminal (was lost in UI rewrite)**
- Observation: The "old footprint" — a bid/ask (net-delta) LADDER anchored at each session's first candle + its imbalance ZONES — disappeared from the Baxter terminal. Rendering code was intact (`TerminalLiveChart.drawFootprint`: Step 1 zones from `sessions[].imbalances`, Step 2 `renderLadder` anchored at `ts.timeToCoordinate(fp.time)` for each session). Root cause was the DATA: `footprintAggregate.aggregateSessions` sums REAL per-candle `levels` only, and real MW data spans just the last ~50 buckets (and the dup-preview bug made that ~15min), so historical sessions had no aggregate → no ladder. The OLD market.tsx (`candleFootprintMap`/`buildAggregate`, market.tsx ~L2233-2335) built these from the OHLCV PROXY (`buildProxyFootprintCandle`) over ALL windowed candles → a ladder on every session. The terminal rewrite dropped proxy.
- Action (HYBRID per user req): `TerminalLiveChart` footprint useEffect builds sessions via a `buildSessions(realByTime?)` helper = `aggregateSessions(validCandles.map(c => realByTime?.get(c.time) ?? buildProxyFootprintCandle({time,open:o,high:h,low:l,close:c})))`. (1) Synchronously builds PROXY-only (`buildSessions()`) so every session shows instantly. (2) The `/api/footprint/history` fetch builds `realByTime` (per-candle real footprint keyed by bucket time) + delta map; if `interval === base && realByTime.size`, REBUILDS sessions with `buildSessions(realByTime)` so candles with real data use real bid/ask levels and the rest stay proxy → real upgrades applicable (recent) sessions, proxy stays for historical. GUARD `interval === base`: real data only exists at 5m, and a 15m chart uses base=5m so 15m candle times wouldn't align 1:1 with 5m buckets (would grab only 1 of 3) — so real merge only on 5m (1m/60m have no real data anyway). `aggregateSessions` handles proxy tiers (PROXY_TIER2/3 when `!FOOTPRINT_DATA_CONFIRMED`). `TerminalCandle` is `{time,o,h,l,c}` (no volume → proxy defaults 100).
- GOTCHA (expected): the ladder for a session only renders when that session's FIRST candle is on-screen (`if (xf<0||xf>cw) continue`) — scroll so the session open (e.g. 9:30 RTH) is visible. Requires Footprint toggle ON + Market/home tab.
- `npm run check` clean. Confidence: high (compile-verified; render mirrors proven market.tsx proxy aggregation)
